市場焦點
美國
科技股
• 美國企業獲利維持強勁，背後主要受經濟展望穩健及AI資本支出熱潮支撐。從獲利來看，市
場共識預期標普500企業EPS成長率2026年達28%，2027年及2028年則分別為20%及16%，
雖呈現逐步降溫，但仍維持雙位數增長。標普500預期本益比已由約23倍降至19倍，且並非
主要來自股價修正，而是企業獲利預期上調所致，在企業獲利維持成長、AI投資持續挹注及
估值已較高點收斂的情況下，標普500仍具中長期配置價值。
美債
外匯對沖視角
• 在全球公債遭遇拋售潮與通膨陰影下，美債因利率期間結構與匯率優勢，今年以美元計價總
回報優於多數G7主權債，儘管海外私人機構投資人正在等待長端利率落底訊號以重返市場，
對美元資金或以美元計價為主的投資人而言，當前美債名目殖利率仍處於數十年高位，具備

相當良好的長期鎖利價值，但價格波動風險考量下，建議債券佈局仍以中短天期為主。
台股
• 8月景氣對策燈號續亮紅燈，綜合判斷分數41分，較前月持平，對應的燈號仍是代表景氣熱絡
的紅燈，連續第9個月亮出紅燈，追平史上最長「連紅」紀錄，顯示國內經濟維持成長。
• 根據歷史統計第四季台股上漲的機率接近八成，除了AI投資趨勢未減，年底集團作帳行情以
及美國11月期中選後不確定性下降有望支撐台股多頭情緒。群益投顧樂觀看待台股第四季表
現，有望上看53,000點。此外，彭博綜合統計台灣加權指數12個月目標價達59,688點。

就業增幅明顯放緩，降低Fed短期再升息急迫性
• 非農明顯降溫，勞動市場尚未出現全面惡化：9月非農僅增2.9萬人，遠低於預期，且7、8月合計下修6萬人；失業率升
至4.2%，但勞參率同步升至61.8%，家戶調查就業增加40.6萬人，顯示失業率上升部分來自勞動供給增加，其中營建、
製造業仍分別增加1.1萬及0.9萬人，平均工時維持34.4小時，數據呈現招聘動能明顯放慢，但尚未出現全面性就業惡化。
• 薪資增速放緩，Fed短期政策壓力下降：9月平均時薪月增僅0.1%、年增3.0%，均低於前值；同時8月PCE年增3.4%、
核心PCE年增3.0%，顯示薪資通膨壓力有所緩和，但整體物價仍高於Fed 2%目標，在9月Fed升息1碼後，非農報告降
低Fed在10月連續升息的必要性，市場焦點轉向年底通膨與AI投資驅動的產業動能。

• 投資建議：短期關注美國中短天期投資級債券，就業降溫與薪資放緩有助降低短端利率上行壓力，但通膨仍高於目標，
因此不宜過度預期長天期利率快速回落；股市則可續關注AI基礎建設與資本支出相關產業，因營建及製造業就業仍具
韌性，顯示企業投資動能尚未明顯轉弱。
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics， 2026.10.3

消費動能回升、通膨低於預期，內需韌性與物價壓力並存
• PCE低於預期、通膨降溫仍待確認：美國8月PCE物價指數月增0.3%、年增3.4%，核心PCE月增0.2%、年增3.0%，均低於
市場預期；其中核心PCE較修訂後7月的3.3%下降，顯示物價壓力有所緩解，但仍高於Fed 2%目標，此次年度更新回溯修
訂2021年以來資料，亦使歷史基期重新調整，後續仍須觀察通膨下降是否能延續。
• 消費回升、所得增幅偏緩：8月個人所得月增0.2%，個人消費支出（PCE）月增0.9%、實質消費成長0.6%，顯示內需仍具
韌性；惟消費增幅明顯高於所得，個人儲蓄率由7月4.6%降至4.1%，為2022年11月以來低點，反映家庭可支應消費的財
務緩衝持續縮小，後續消費動能仍須觀察。
• 後市觀察：PCE低於預期後，市場對10月再升息的預期明顯降溫，但長端美債殖利率仍受通膨、財政供給與期限溢價牽動

而維持高檔；建議可聚焦獲利成長與現金流穩健的優質大型成長股與短天期美債以掌握收益，同時留意儲蓄率下滑與9月
消費者信心低迷可能抑制後續消費動能，動態調整部位並嚴控風險。
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Economic Analysis， 2026.10.1
PCE與核心PCE年增率(%)走勢 八月消費支出各類別分項比較

顧
能源與AI推升長端利率，融資成本壓力延續
• 長端殖利率上行、期限溢價成重要推力：近期
美債呈現短端與長端走勢分化，雖市場對10月
Fed再升息預期因8月PCE低於預期而明顯降溫，
10年期及30年期的美債殖利率仍偏高位，反映
通膨風險、財政融資需求與期限溢價上升，長
天期無風險利率維持高檔，並推升房貸及企業
融資成本。
• 能源與AI投資推升通膨及資金需求：油價維持
高檔增加通膨黏著風險，AI基礎建設投資則帶
動企業資本支出與融資需求，兩者共同提高長
期資金需求；在財政赤字與美債供給仍大的環
境下，長端殖利率可能維持較高水準，進而提
高股票估值折現率及高槓桿企業的融資壓力。
• 後市觀察：短期持續觀察油價、10年及30年期
美債殖利率與期限溢價變化；債券配置仍建議
降低長天期債券部位以降低利率風險，優先布

局短天期高品質債券獲取穩健息收，股票則聚
焦具穩健現金流、低財務槓桿與定價能力的企
業，以降低高利率環境下的估值與融資風險。
資料來源：Bloomberg，Federal Reserve Bank of New Tork， 2026.10.1
長天期美債殖利率曲線與期限溢價變動趨勢

美光最新一季財報與財測 2026會計年度Q4實際 市場預期 2027會計年度Q1財測
營收 542.3億美元 約504.5-510.7億美元 615億美元 ±15億美元
調整後EPS 33.42美元 約31.16-31.72美元 38.15美元 ±1美元
調整後毛利率 87.0% - 約86.25%
調整後營業利益 446.4億美元 - -
調整後淨利 384.0億美元 - -
營運現金流 439.73億美元 - -
AI需求 強勁 - 預期持續成長
HBM需求 產能供不應求 - 持續擴產
自由現金流 590億美元 - -
AI需求大增，美光財測超過市場預期
• AI記憶體需求強勁，美光財測顯示景氣延續：美光科技9/30盤後公布財報，2026財年的第四季營收達542.3億美元，年增

379%，調整後EPS 33.42美元，均優於市場預期；公司並預估2027會計年度第一季營收615億美元、調整後EPS 38.15美元，
分別高於市場預期的567.7億美元及36.02美元。強勁財測反映AI資料中心持續推升HBM、伺服器DRAM及高速SSD需求。
隨Amazon、Alphabet等大型科技企業持續擴大AI基礎建設投資，記憶體需求仍具成長動能。
• 長約與產品升級，支撐中長期業績：美光目前已簽署36份多年期供應協議，涵蓋約三分之一營收並延伸至2030年，有助降
低記憶體產業週期波動；同時持續布局HBM4、HBM4E及高容量DDR5等產品。公司2026會計年度自由現金流達590億美元，
並預期2027、2028會計年度供需將進一步緊俏。整體而言，AI需求已由單一HBM擴展至伺服器記憶體與儲存產品，美光財
測亦顯示景氣延續性仍強，後續市場焦點將轉向擴產速度、資本支出及AI需求成長能否維持。
資料來源：美光，彭博，鉅亨網，2026.10.1

市
場
回
顧
• AI驅動獲利成長，企業基本面仍具支撐：美國企業獲利維持強勁，背後主要受經濟展望穩健及AI資本支出熱潮支撐。從獲利
來看，市場共識預期標普500企業EPS成長率2026年達28%，2027年及2028年則分別為20%及16%，雖呈現逐步降溫，但
仍維持雙位數增長。其中，AI基礎建設與大型雲端業者仍是重要驅動來源，2026年對EPS成長的貢獻合計約19%，2027、
2028年仍分別貢獻14%及11%。
• 獲利上修降低估值壓力：標普500預期本益比已由約23倍降至19倍，且並非主要來自股價修正，而是企業獲利預期上調所致。
雖然未來EPS成長率若由高檔回落，市場仍可能面臨評價修正壓力，但在企業獲利維持成長、AI投資持續挹注及估值已較高
點收斂的情況下，標普500基本面仍具支撐。因此，短期應留意獲利增速放緩與市場持倉偏高所帶來的波動，但從獲利成長

與估值兩大面向觀察，標普500仍具中長期配置價值。
AI驅動獲利成長，企業基本面仍具支撐

生產回升與價格上漲同步推動中國PMI改善
• 生產回升與價格上漲同步推動中國PMI改善： 9月製造業PMI升至50.1%，較前月回升0.3個百分點，重返榮枯線以上，顯示
製造業景氣有所改善，主要受生產活動回暖及價格上漲帶動，中小企業景氣同步改善，但仍低於榮枯線，大型企業表現相對
較佳，高技術製造、裝備製造維持擴張，消費品PMI升至50.7%，重回景氣區間，另一方面，原材料及能源價格上漲推升企
業成本，上下游獲利差距持續擴大。 非製造業PMI則由49.0%升至50.2%，服務業受節慶效應帶動回溫，建築業則受財政資

金投入，及基建建設推進而改善，不過需求復甦仍偏溫和，企業成本壓力尚未明顯減輕。
• 結論：第四季隨著基建投資、財政支出及房市支持政策逐步落地，經濟可望獲得支撐，但10月受長假因素影響，PMI可能短
期承壓。

台灣景氣燈號連9紅 台股Q4有望突破5萬點大關
• 8月景氣對策燈號續亮紅燈：綜合判斷分數41分，較前月持平，對應的燈號仍是代表景氣熱絡的紅燈，連續第9個月亮出紅
燈，追平史上最長「連紅」紀錄，顯示國內經濟維持成長。國發會表示，AI需求令台廠積極擴廠，加上政府推動AI新十大建
設，民間投資有望維持動能。此外，受惠財富效應、企業調薪及就業市場穩定，民間消費有望持續成長。
• 法人看好台股年底前站上5萬點：根據歷史統計第四季台股上漲的機率接近八成，除了AI投資趨勢未減，年底集團作帳行情
以及美國11月期中選後不確定性下降有望支撐台股多頭情緒。群益投顧樂觀看待台股第四季表現，有望上看53,000點。此
外，彭博綜合統計台灣加權指數12個月目標價達59,688點。
• 觀點：近期台股站上48,000點整數關卡，總市值達5.3兆美元，讓台股耀升成為全球第四大市值股市，顯示台股在AI浪潮下

的得天獨厚。年底前仍須留意中東衝突進展，若能源通膨壓力持續，聯準會升息預期恐為股市帶來波動，但考慮AI科技持續
發酵，建議投資人續抱台股，並以定期不定額的策略紀律加碼，目標追求中長期完整的AI趨勢回報。
資料來源：國發會，2026.09.29。

外匯對沖視角—美債對沖後殖利率屬全球最低之一
• 美國國債殖利率真的高嗎？在當前全球總體經濟環境下，美國國債殖利率雖維持在數十年歷史高點附近，但若單憑名目殖利
率來判斷美債的吸引力，容易忽視匯率對沖與各國財政風險帶來的溢價差異。
• 美元視角下的殖利率比較：若將其他已開發市場的主權債務(如德債、日債、英債)完全對沖回美元，美債在殖利率曲線上多
數期限的實際殖利率，其實與德債並列全球最低水準。相反地，日本國債與英國國債經匯率對沖回美元後的收益率反而更高。
• 跨貨幣資產交換(ASW)的真實回報：以10年期主權債的對沖跨貨幣資產交換計算：美國10年期國債有效殖利率約4.85%，
日本10年期國債(對沖至美元)有效殖利率約5.06%，德國10年期國債(對沖至美元)有效殖利率約4.51%，這表明美國政府發

債的真實對沖融資成本其實已相對精實，且符合國際市場對各國財政基本面的合理定價。
對沖至美元後的10年期主權債殖利率
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.29

財政基本面與信用風險—殖利率溢價反映國家債務比重
• 從歐元計價或信用風險的角度來比較，美債的定價邏輯非常符合總體經濟基本面：儘管債務以歐元計價時，10年期美債殖
利率比同期限德國國債高出約30個基點，這反映出德國的債務佔GDP比重僅約65%，而美國則高達127%，因此美債承擔了
適度的財政溢價。儘管義大利的債務佔GDP比重高達140%且信用前景較遜色，但在統一貨幣基準下，義大利國債殖利率仍
較美國國債高出60個基點，這顯示市場並未過度壓低美債信用，美債依然享有優質避險資產的溢價優勢。
• 海外私人投資者的資金流向：近一年海外私人機構在美債加碼速度放緩，乃通膨環境下全球其他資產選擇增加，及過去升息
波段導致帳面按市值計價虧損。然而歐洲投資人(持有一半以上的海外美債)目前仍持有龐大部位，並在等待適當的加碼時機。

• 小結與投資建議：在經過外匯對沖後，美債收益率在發達國家主權債中處於相對低位，發行成本合理且具競爭力。美債殖利
率高於德債低於義債，精確地反映美國政府債務負擔率(127%)與總體經濟穩健度。在全球公債遭遇拋售潮與通膨陰影下，
美債因利率期間結構與匯率優勢，今年以美元計價總回報優於多數G7主權債，儘管海外私人機構投資人正在等待長端利率
落底訊號以重返市場，對美元資金或以美元計價為主的投資人而言，當前美債名目殖利率仍處於數十年高位，具備相當良好
的長期鎖利價值，但價格波動風險考量下，建議債券佈局仍以中短天期為主。
歐元計價美債殖利率高於德但低於義
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.29

亞洲晶⽚債券跑輸指數，評級保持穩健
• 亞洲晶⽚債券跑輸指數，評級保持穩健：儘管AI熱潮推動收⼊創下紀錄，但SK海力⼠和台積電的美元債，今年以來的表現仍
略遜於彭博亞洲美元信用債總回報指數，主因為大規模資本支出擴張，以及潛在的投⼊資本回報率下降令其利差承壓，美國
雲服務巨頭創紀錄的AI支出計畫加劇了壓力，儘管如此SK海力⼠和台積電仍保持較強的高評級信用實力，台積電計畫投
⼊600-640億美元資本支出，SK海力⼠的資本支出則處於40兆韓圜區間。兩家公司穩健的資產負債表、合計共1,260億美元
的淨現金、強勁的經營性現金流，以及在先進製程，和HBM晶⽚領域的市場領先地位，為上述支出提供支撐。
• 投資建議：現階段台積電與SK海力⼠美元債最大的問題不是信用風險，而是估值與利差壓力，短期可能因AI資本支出過大，

及市場情緒波動而表現落後指數，但以兩家公司強大的現金流、淨現金部位及產業領導地位來看，信用品質仍屬亞洲投資級
債中的優質標的，建議利差擴大時分批布局 。

顧 • 企業獲利成長將支撐股價上行：標普500指數的長期走勢與企業每股盈餘（EPS）高度連動。高盛預估，標普500企業EPS將
於2026年達375美元，並在2027年進一步升至415美元，對應年增率約36%與11%。在利率維持高檔、金融條件偏緊的環境
下，企業獲利仍持續處於成長軌道，將為股市提供穩健的中期上行動能。
• 本益比回落提供潛在布局契機：根據FactSet數據，標普500企業第三季整體獲利預計成長30.6%。其中，資訊科技類股獲利
成長預估高達66.5%，領先能源以外的其他產業；同時，該類股預估本益比已由今年高點回落，在獲利上修與估值修正同步
發生的背景下，浮現具吸引力的中期布局機會。
• 小結：短期市場仍可能受地緣政治與利率預期擾動，但中期趨勢仍由企業獲利擴張所主導。建議投資人採取分批布局科技股

策略，聚焦具備AI受益結構、資本支出能見度高及現金流穩健的龍頭企業。

非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。
警 語

## Document 099: 2026年・冬のボーナス予測 ～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年・冬のボーナス予測 ～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年 11 月 6 日

1/2 
2026 年・冬のボーナス予測 
～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

○ 民間企業の2026 年冬のボーナス一人当たり支給額を前年比＋3.0％（昨冬同＋2.8％）と予想する
（厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」ベース）。冬のボーナスとしては６年連続の増加となるだろう。中
東情勢の悪化に伴う資源価格上昇が企業活動の重荷となるなかでも企業収益は高水準を維持してい
る。人手不足を背景とした処遇改善の動きも根強く、今冬もボーナスの増加が続くと予想する。

○ 11 月６日に厚生労働省から公表された26 年夏のボーナス（賞与支給事業所における労働者一人平
均賞与額）は、前年比＋5.3％と５年連続で増加し、昨夏の同＋2.9％から伸びも拡大した。また、
賞与を支給していない事業所も含む「全事業所における労働者一人平均賞与額」で見ても同＋
5.9％と増加している。賞与を支給する事業所の割合も前年の 73.4％から74.0％へ上昇しており、
企業の従業員への還元姿勢が強いことがうかがえる。

○ ボーナスの交渉では、春闘時にその年の年間賞与を決定する夏冬型が採用されていることが多い。
2026 年春闘では、月例給与だけでなく一時金でも増額回答が目立った。企業収益が高水準にある
ことに加え、物価高への配慮や人材確保の観点から、賞与を含めた処遇改善を進める企業が多かっ
たとみられる。また、月例給与の引き上げが賞与の算定基礎額を押し上げ、支給月数の増加と相ま
ってボーナス増につながる面もある。夏のボーナス増に続き、冬についてもこうした交渉結果が反
映される形で増加が予想される。

○ 毎期型の企業、あるいは労働組合が存在しない中小・零細企業においては、より直近の収益状況・
業況がボーナスに反映されやすい。もっとも、イラン情勢悪化によるコスト上昇が企業収益の下押
し要因となるなかでも、価格転嫁の進展などを背景として企業収益は総じて底堅く推移している。
利益の水準も高く、従業員への還元余力はなお保たれているとみてよいだろう。

○ 人手不足感が強いことも、ボーナス増加を後押しする。企業にとって人材の確保・定着は引き続き
重要な経営課題となっており、月例給与だけでなく賞与を含めた処遇改善の必要性は高い。物価高
によって家計負担が増加するなか、従業員への還元を求める声も強い。こうした点も今冬のボーナ
ス増加を支える要因となる。

○ 労務行政研究所が９月30 日に公表した調査によれば、東証プライム上場企業 175 社の 2026 年年末

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
2/2 
一時金妥結額は、前年比＋4.2％と５年連続で増加した。製造業が同＋4.3％、非製造業が同＋
3.5％と、いずれもプラスとなっている。大企業中心の調査であり、中小企業やパートタイム労働
者も含む毎月勤労統計とは対象が異なるものの、冬のボーナスも堅調に増加するという見方を裏付
ける結果といえる。

○ 冬のボーナス増加は家計の所得環境にとって好材料であり、個人消費を下支えすることが期待され
る。一方で懸念されるのが物価上昇の影響である。中東情勢悪化に伴うコスト上昇の価格転嫁など
を背景として、今後は物価上昇率が高まる可能性が高く、政府による電気・ガス代支援の効果を織
り込んでも、2027 年初にはＣＰＩコアの前年比上昇率が＋３％台に達することが予想される。物
価上昇による家計の購買力の圧迫が続くなか、ボーナス増加による個人消費の押し上げ効果につい
ては過度な期待は禁物だろう。

（出所）厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」
（注）26年冬の予測値は第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所

## Document 120: 261109_daiwa_ Giant Manufacturing

Source PDF: all report pdf/261109_daiwa_ Giant Manufacturing.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: Giant announced its 3Q26 results after market hours on 9 
November 2026. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating on its improving 
fundamentals and the recovery of the global bike market. We believe 
inventory destocking is coming to an end.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results: missed on operating margin. Giant’s 
3Q26 revenue of TWD16.311bn (-2.1% QoQ, +6.2% YoY) was roughly in 
line with our estimate and the Bloomberg consensus. Its gross margin of 
23.9% beat our estimate and the Bloomberg consensus by 2.9pp and 
1.1pp, respectively, driven by product mix optimisation and new product 
launches in its own brand. However, its operating margin of 3.1% missed 
our estimate and the consensus by 1.4pp and 2.1pp, respectively, due to 
marketing activities for MY27 launches and legal fees related to the WRO. 
Thus, its 3Q26 operating profit of TWD510m (-49.0% QoQ, +28.9% YoY) 
missed our estimate and the consensus by 31.3% and 42.2%, respectively. 
Its 3Q26 net profit of TWD330m (-48.0% QoQ, -5.7% YoY) also missed our 
estimate and the consensus by 39.6% and 47.8%, respectively. Its 3Q26 
EPS finished at TWD0.84.

Demand recovery in Europe and China; a bright 2027. Giant sees 
gradual market recovery in Europe on new model launches and demand 
recovery in China. In the US, Giant continues to work towards having the 
WRO lifted, but will adjust production allocation to maximise the supply to 
meet demand in the US market. Its E-bike revenue accounted for 21% of 
9M26 revenue, which we believe reflects the declining OEM segment. 
Giant aims for its e-bike shipments to decline by 1% YoY, from 516,000 
units in 2025 to 510,000 in 2026, on weaker OE customer demand. For 
2026, it guides for sales to decline by low-single-digit % YoY, with the gross 
margin improving to 21-23% (from 20-21%) and an operating margin of 1-
3%. We lower our 2026-28E EPS by 5-30% after factoring in the 3Q26 
results with more conservative operating-margin estimates on marketing 
expenses.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call with an unchanged 
12M TP of TWD121, still based on our 1-year forward EPS forecast with an 
unchanged target PER of 22x to reflect its improved earnings trajectory, 
and we look for a better year in 2027. Key downside risks: slower-than-
expected e-bike penetration in the EU and macro/tariff uncertainties.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 8-26% below the consensus, likely 
as our revenue and operating margin forecasts are more conservative.

9 November 2026

3Q26 results review: missed on operating margin

➢ 3Q26 net profit missed our estimate and the consensus by 39-48% 
➢ Better-than-expected recovery in Europe and China; a brighter 2027 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD121

Company profile 
Founded in 1972 and listed in 1994 on Taiwan’s main board, Giant is the largest bicycle maker in 
Taiwan, with an annual capacity of c.5m bicycles. In 2025, Asia accounted for 57% of its revenue, 
followed by Europe (33%) and America (7%). Around 74% of its 2025 revenue came from OBM (Giant, 
Liv, momentum) brands and 26% from OEM/ODM (Trek, Scott and Colnago) brands.

### Page 5

5

Giant Manufacturing (9921 TT): 9 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 7 Aug 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and Chairperson are not the same individual. Led by the Liu family, there are 11
members on the board. The founder and former Chairman is now a director, with his niece, Mrs.
Tu, leading Giant. We view Mrs. Tu's leadership to be strong, as she is well-recognised by the
former Chairman and the board for her capabilities, and she is one of the few female
chairpersons to lead a Taiwanese company.
Capital management 2 Giant has maintained its dividend payout ratio between 50% and 100% over the past decade , which we believe to be reasonable considering its global leadership status and expansion needs.
Related party &
transaction 2
Revenue from related-party transactions accounted for 58-62% of total revenue over 2019-25,
with sales mostly from its OBM (ASP varies with products/markets). We observed no suspicious
activity.
S Product quality & safety 1
According to Giant's 2023 ESG report, Giant as the global OEM/OBM leader in the bicycle
industry, product quality and safety are the top priority. We see ongoing R&D expenses and
relevant investment in new models and note that Giant has introduced its "Right Ride" system to
assist customers seeking suitable bike options.
S Supply chain management 1
Giant has developed its Giant production management system that diversifies its capacity
globally and utilises production resources fully to lower political or regional risk. As Giant has
control over its material sourcing, design process and manufacturing/OEM, we believe it has
solid supply chain management capabilities that allow for better cost control than peers.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

Asia Vital Components

Solid 3Q26 results; positive tone to 4Q26/ 
2027; reiterate Buy; trim PO to NT$3,650

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 3,650 TWD | Price: 2,790 TWD

3Q26: record-high GM/OPM for five straight quarters 
Asia Vital Components (AVC)’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 33.9%/28.5% hit a record high for the 
fifth straight quarter, exceeding BofAe of 33.0%/27.5% and consensus of 33.3%/27.9%. 
OP (+25% QoQ, +108% YoY) was 7%/4% above BofAe/consensus, with higher sales 
(+20% QoQ, +52% YoY; 3%/2% above BofAe/consensus). We attribute the results beat 
to solid product mix (server and networking comprised 66% of 3Q26 sales, same as 1Q-
2Q26) and improving production efficiency. Despite a non-operating loss (mainly FX 
loss), NP was 10%/3% above consensus (Exhibit 3).  
Positive tone to 4Q26/2027; ongoing capacity expansion 
We expect 4Q26 sales to rise 19% QoQ and 2027 sales to grow 40% YoY, with mgmt.’s 
still positive tone to 4Q26/2027, mainly driven by AI liquid cooling projects. AVC 
indicated full support to clients’ platform upgrade and shipment pipeline. It should be 
able to pass through the higher raw material cost. In 2027, mgmt. targets ASIC sales mix 
to surpass GPU. AVC also sees solid sales momentum from chassis/rack and CDU, acting 
as the solution provider. Mgmt. maintained 2026 capex guidance at NT$15bn and guided 
NT$15-20bn for 2027 (BofAe unchanged at NT$17bn), mainly for capacity expansion in 
Vietnam. Mgmt. now targets another 50% cold plate capacity expansion in 2027. 
Trim PO to NT$3,650 for a lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We lift 2026-28E EPS by 3-10%, mainly to reflect the 3Q26 results beat and higher 
margins, driven by ongoing server/networking project ramp-up. Yet, we cut our PO to 
NT$3,650 (23x 2027E EPS), with a lower P/E multiple (2SD above average since 2020; 
Exhibit 5) from NT$3,888 (was 27x; 3SD above) on AI supply chain’s potentially higher 
shipment/pipeline uncertainty due to component shortage. Nevertheless, we reiterate 
Buy on AVC as we believe it should remain one of the key beneficiaries from rising 
liquid-cooling adoption and ongoing GPU/ASIC platform/spec upgrade. We also highlight 
solid sales/margin growth under broad product base and diversified project/client base.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
AVC is the leading system-level thermal/cooling component 
maker globally. Key end markets include server, PC, 
smartphone. Key end clients include global CSPs, PC brands, 
smartphone brands. AVC was founded in 1991 and went 
publicly listed on Taiwan Stock Exchange on 27 Sep 2002. It 
is headquartered in Taiwan. Its main production sites are 
located in China (Shenzhen, Wuhan, Shanghai, Dongguan, 
Chengdu), with ongoing capacity expansion in Vietnam.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to AVC given its leading position in 
thermal/cooling component and total solution strategy to 
ride on strong AI market demand, double-digit sales/EPS 
CAGR in 2026-28 with rising liquid cooling sales mix and 
ASP/margin increase.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Asia Vital Components (AVMPF) 
Our PO of NT$3,650 is based on 23x 2027E EPS. We use 23x P/E multiple (2SD above 
P/E average since 2020) in view of AVC's double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E. We 
believe AVC's leading position in thermal/cooling component market and strong AI 
market demand will drive rising liquid cooling sales mix and ASP/margin increase.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) stronger demand in AI server, server, PC, smartphone, (2) 
better operating leverage with larger revenue scale, (3) less intensive competition, (4) 
faster product certification, (5) faster capacity expansion, (6) favorable FX movement 
(USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to help on GM and/or non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/component 
shortage, (2) weaker server/PC/smartphone market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (3) GM pressure due to higher material/labor/utility cost, (4) more 
intensive competition, (5) delayed product certification, (6) slower capacity expansion, 
(7) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-
operating items.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line

We introduce 2027 forecast at 90.4K racks (+10% y/y) vs. our 2026 forecast
now at 82K racks: This year is a strong one for downstream rack assemblers, with
rack shipments up ~209% y/y, but we expect rack shipments to grow another ~10%
y/y to reach 90.4K in 2027. For 2027, our forecast implies Hon Hai will be a share
gainer with rack share up to 43% (from 37% in 2026), with Quanta staying
unchanged at 26% and Wistron's share up slightly to 24% (from 23% in 2026).

Quanta (2382.TW, OW): October revenue was NT$353.3bn (+9% m/m, +104% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth mainly to higher GB200/300 rack shipments at ~1.8K
racks (+24% m/m), despite being offset by lower NB shipments at 2.7M units (-36%
m/m, -23% y/y). For 2026, we forecast Quanta to ship 20.9K racks, and our 2027
rack production forecast for Quanta is at 23.5K (+12% y/y).
Wistron (3231.TW, OW): October revenue was  NT$402.6B (+6% m/m, +108% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth to: 1) higher GB200/300 compute tray shipments

(1.60K rack equivalents, by our estimates, up ~22% m/m), despite lower Wiwynn
revenue at ~NT$165.1bn (-4% m/m, +81% y/y) and lower NB shipments at 1.2mn units
(-20% m/m, -43% y/y), flat DT shipments at 500K (flat m/m, -44% y/y), and lower
monitor shipments at 850K (-11% m/m, -6% y/y).  For 2026, we forecast Wistron to
ship 18.7K racks, and our 2027 rack production forecast is at 22K (+18% y/y).
Hon Hai (2317.TW, OW): October revenue was NT$1,305.3bn (+13% m/m, +46% y/y),

with consumer segment revenue seeing the strongest m/m growth, followed by
computing products, and lastly, components and other products, while cloud and
networking products were down m/m due to a rise in consignment mix; however,
overall rack production volumes are up m/m. We model HH's October GB200/300
rack production at 2.9K (+7% m/m). For 2026, we forecast Foxconn to ship 30.3K
racks, and our rack production 2027 forecast at 38.8K (+28% y/y).

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We estimate GB200/300 rack output in October  at ~7.5K (+16% m/m).
Quanta shipped ~1.80K  GB200/GB300 racks.
Wistron shipped ~1.60K GB200/GB300 racks.
Hon Hai shipped ~2.90K GB200/300 racks.
We keep our 2027 rack production forecast unchanged at 90.4K racks (+11% y/y).

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

ab 10 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
ChipMOS Technologies
Q326 earnings ahead on better GM; optics and
AI ASIC testing support 2027E growth
Strong margin recovery drives Q326 EPS beat
Q326 EPS came in ahead at NT$1.89 vs. UBSe/street of NT$1.27/1.47 due to higher
margins. Q326 sales was already posted through monthly sales at NT$8.4bn, +14%
QoQ,  supported  by robust  demand from memory (51% of sales, +13% QoQ) on
improving commodity DRAM volume and better momentum from driver IC due to OLED

iPhone ramp  (27% of DDIC sales, +46% QoQ), while it also raised memory backend ASP
to reflect rising component cost. Q326 GM improved to 21.7% (+3.7ppt QoQ) on better
pricing, lower pass through for bumping and higher loading, and  exceeded our estimate
of 18.6%. Q326 OPEX remains well controlled at 6.4% of sales, while it also booked
one-off other operating income of NT$373mn, resulting in better OPM of 19.7%.
Memory and OLED momentum to sustain into Q426

ChipMOS sees better momentum for memory over driver IC business into Q426F  on
continued strength from commodity DRAM and improving NAND Flash outlook. For its
DDIC/bumping business, despite softer end-demand for consumer electronics,
particularly PC and Android smartphone, ChipMOS noted the seasonal build for OLED
iPhone should provide some cushion for its driver IC sales. Management noted it will
continue to optimize its product portfolio and divest lower-end capacity, while

negotiates with its customers on pass through higher component cost if needed. We
forecast ChipMOS's Q426E sales to grow 2% QoQ, with GM expanding by 0.3ppt QoQ
to 22.0% on better  product mix and normalized electricity cost.
Optics and AI ASIC testing support 2027E growth
Management noted its copper bumping for US customers' next-generation products
remain under qualification and expects to see more meaningful revenue contribution by
late-2027F or 2028F. In the meantime, it is  working with the customer on producing

some legacy products in 2027F, although revenue contribution remains limited. As
optics-related business carries significantly better pricing than traditional DDIC
bumping, ChipMOS will prioritize its driver IC/bumping resource for optics and OLED
DDI, instead for Korean customer's outsourcing. Separately, ChipMOS continues to
expand its ASIC testing roadmap, having already entered mass production for
smartphone AP and expecting to progress to smartphone SoC testing, followed by ASIC

final test opportunities if the current program ramps successfully through H127.
Valuation: Reiterate Buy rating with price target of NT$155
We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 6-23% on  improving GM profile driven by more favorable
mix but trim our PT from NT$166 to NT$155 as we apply a lower multiple of 20x 2027-
28E average PE (from 23x) given the recent market valuation contraction. Reiterate Buy
rating on improving margin profiles new AI (optics and logic testing) initiatives.

Company Description
ChipMOS is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 45-50% of its sales from display driver
IC (~40% from TV and 40% from smartphone and IT and 20% from automotive), 35-40%
from memory (~40% DRAM, ~40% from specialty flash and ~20% from mainstream NAND)
and 10-15% from logic (TV SoC) and mixed signal IC (MEMS, PMIC).

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for ChipMOS is based on a target PE multiple. Downside risks include a
weaker-than-expected tech end-market demand recovery, growing China competition,
failure to diversify into the SiPh/optical component bumping business and a slower ramp of its
logic testing business.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

governance (ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of

Wiwynn (6669.TW): ASIC AI servers and General servers to drive growth 
ahead; 3Q26 and Oct in line; Buy
13 November 2026 | 8:50PM HKT
Wiwynn Oct revenues were in line with our estimates at NT$159bn, driven by solid 
general servers growth and its major ASIC customers’ new generation of ASIC AI 
servers (air-cooling) entering mass production since June 2026. Looking ahead, we 
model sequential revenue increase in Nov and Dec, considering liquid-cooled ASIC AI 
servers of its major customers, and as we expect GPU AI server racks ramp up in 
4Q26E. We expect 4Q26 revenues growth to remain strong, up to +15% QoQ/ 71% 
YoY, accelerating further to +38% QoQ in 1Q27, driven by AI servers’ new models 
ramp up, AI servers’ expansion from ASIC to GPU, potential ASIC AI servers SKU 
expansion, and less impact from the general server Buy & Sell to Consignment shift. 
Maintain Buy with TP unchanged at NT$4,000 (19.2x 2027E target PE vs. +37% EPS 
YoY in 2027-28E).

3Q26 results in line: Revenues is pre-announced; GM declined to 8.9%, in line with 
our estimates, while better than Bloomberg consensus of 8.4%, leading GP to +51% 
QoQ, in line with our estimates, or 13% ahead of Bloomberg consensus. The lower 
GM reflects AI servers ramp up, along with less NRE as new models entering mass

production. Meanwhile, opex ratio was down to 1.5%, largely in line with our / 
Bloomberg consensus of 1.6%, leading OP income to +61% QoQ, or 2% / 16% ahead of 
our / Bloomberg consensus. The lower opex ratio is mainly driven by lower SG&A 
expenses reflecting improved operational efficiency as the company expands its AI 
servers, with potential ASIC AI servers SKUs expansion, and AI servers transition from 
ASIC-powered to GPU-powered.

Earnings revision: We factor in 3Q26 results and Oct revenues, and change our net 
income by 1% / +6% / +0% in 2026-28E. The change is mainly on revenues and opex 
ratio: (1) we raise 2026-27E revenues by 2% / 2%, reflecting the ramp up of new AI 
server products, and the company’s potential of new ASIC AI servers customers 
expansion. (2) We cut 2026-27E opex ratio by 0.1 ppts/ 0.2 ppts, reflecting improved 
operation efficiency as the company expands its AI servers. We keep our 2028E 
estimates largely unchanged.

### Page 3

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12M TP based on near-term P/E, and our 2027E 
target P/E is slightly cut to 19.2x, which is still derived from the peers’ correlation of 
forward trading P/E and EPS growth. Our 12M TP is unchanged at NT$4,000. Our target 
P/E of 19.2x is above the company’s historical avg.+1stv. forward trading P/E, reflecting 
our positive view on Wiwynn’s leading position in ASIC AI server ODM along with product 
expansion (from ASIC to GPU AI servers) and customers expansion. Maintain Buy.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$4,000. Our target price is 
based on 19.2x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 13 Nov 2026 close.

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部調升世芯-KY 的投資建議為強力買進，目標價上修至 6850
元，基於以下五點理由：(1)3Q26 雖受到供應鏈緊張影響，營收仍
持續創高，(2)4Q26 營收將持續攀升，(3)27 年 CSP 3 代晶片貢獻
營收幅度上修，(4)新增 AI 晶片客戶，有望在 27 年下半年貢獻約
10 億美元營收，(5)CSP 4 代晶片更加確立，28 年營收更高層級的
營收，且費用控管得宜，OPM 純度持續增加。綜合以上，維持 26 年
EPS 預估為 120.31 元(原估120.3 元，+65.5%YOY)，上修 27 年 EPS
預估為 198.3 元(原估 178.99 元，+64.8%YOY)，及預估 28 年 EPS
為 341.95 元(+72.5%YOY)。雖然 1H26 營運動能表現不佳，但最差
狀況確實已過，目前供應鏈因素逐步排除，且在 27 年 CSP 客戶的
訂單配比增加，且已經證明世芯為北美 CSP 客戶唯一後段設計服務
供應商的角色，新增客戶產品也將在 27 年貢獻，先前股價修正幅
度較大，故上修建議至強力買進，目標價上修至 6850 元(27 年
EPS*35XPER OR 28 年EPS*20XPER)。

重點摘要：

1. 3Q26 受到供應鏈影響，惟營收仍維持高檔：  
世芯-KY 3Q26 營收為 261.9 億元(原估 284.5 億元)，成長
242.8%QOQ，成長 298.7%YOY，小幅不如原先預期主要係因
載板及封裝產能稀缺等負面影響，如產能有爭取夠將有望
增加約 1 億美元之營收，就與先前預估相當。毛利率下滑
至 17.95%，與原先預估 17.5%相差不大，主要是部分 NRE
有遞延認列影響。費用 10.7 億元，稍微高於於預估，不過
整體稅後淨利仍達到 32.51 億元(原估 33.09 億元)，成長
98.5%QOQ，成長 145%YOY，EPS 37.67 元(原估 39.28 元)，
差異來自於營收規模。

2. 4Q26 營收將續創新高： 
展望 4Q26，雖然在法說會上公司未提及財測數字預估，不
過 CSP 客戶 3nm 案件持續放量，先前有供應端材料不順的
影響，與供應商及客戶偕同持續排除下，推估營收有望續
創新高。故研究部維持 4Q26 營收預估為 334.5 億元，成長
27.7%QOQ，成長 607.6%YOY，毛利率與先前預估相當的
17.5%，稅後淨利預估為 40.66 億元(原估 39.1 億元)，成
長 25.1%QOQ，成長 174.2%YOY，EPS 47.11 元(原估 45.35
元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. CSP 3 代晶片推估 26 年貢獻 15 億美金，27 年確定上修 
法說會雖然因法規及與客戶 NDA 的關係，皆無提到 CSP 客
戶三代晶片能夠貢獻的營收幅度，不過從營收可以看出，
先前規劃的投片持續順利產出，26 年維持貢獻 15 億美元
營收不變。展望 27 年，先前預估較為保守，主要係因 3nm
產能稀缺，但此次法說會公司表示，在研發上持續配合客
戶，而客戶依賴度提升狀況下，27 年的訂單配比有所提升，
產能也有所確保，故上修成長幅度至 100%YOY 以上，先前
僅預估 60-70%YOY 左右。

4. CSP 4 代晶片訂單更加確立，且新增另一客戶： 
另外在 4 代晶片方面，法說會當中有提到 2nm 案件將在
4Q26 TAPE OUT，且再次宣告也只有一家後段設計服務供
應商，包含 IO DIE 及連結部分，且公司也扮演整合晶片的
角色，以時間點來看，將有望在 4Q27 做小規模的量產，且
僅有 ANNAPURNA 及世芯有在台積電投片該晶片的權力。 
此次在法說會有提到，公司新增另外一個大客戶，公司未
透露細節，但非 GOOGLE，客戶為美國客戶，晶片就是與 AI
相關(未指出是推論還是訓練)，且該晶片在 27 年下半年
會貢獻營收，推估營收規模將達到 10 億美元。而世芯與該
客戶的生意將具有延續性。 
故綜合以上，世芯對於 28 年營收規模期待將有更大的跳
升，且 OPM 將會往 HIGH TEENS 邁進。

5. 26年約略維持預估，27年預估上修，28年粗估高度成長： 
3Q26世芯財報約略符合預期，雖然有供應鏈相關問題，不
過4Q26營收仍將續創新高，故維持26年營收預估為714.6
億元(原估737億元)，EPS 120.31元(原估120.3元)。展望
27年，CSP客戶三代晶片貢獻全年，加上爭取到的投片及訂
單配比上修，另外新增客戶也將貢獻營收，故上修營收為
1529.3億元(原估1335億元)，EPS上修至198.3元(原估
178.99元)。28年在CSP 4代晶片開始貢獻下，研究部粗估
1Q28小幅量產，2Q28開始大幅放量，推估28年營收將達到
2593億元，成長72.5%YOY，EPS預估再跳升至341.95元。

貿聯-KY (3665 TT) 
併購綜效超預期，獲利動能再加速

永豐觀點 
併購 Interplex、新富生貢獻雙雙超預期，獲利上修循環啟動。 
 投資評價與建議 
重申買進建議：研究部本次上修貿聯財務預估：(1) Interplex 資料通訊業務
(Datacom Business)營收規模大幅超標：10 月首個完整併表月營收約新台幣 21
億元，大幅優於研究部原先預期之約 13 億元，研究部同步上修其 2026~2028 年
營收與獲利貢獻預估。(2) 新富生月營收規模自年初以來已成長逾一倍，憑藉資料
中心高階光纖跳線的製造與品質優勢，持續拓展新客戶，研究部預估其 2027 年營
收可持續倍增。(3) 產品組合轉佳、營運槓桿持續發揮：3Q26 毛利率季增 1.8 個
百點至 32.53%，且營業費用率降至約 12%，帶動營益率創 20.78%新高。綜合預
估貿聯 2026-2028 年集團營收分別為 1,074、1,659、2,161 億元(+51%、+55%、
+30%YoY)；稅後淨利 150、267、356 元(+66%、+79%、+33%YoY)，完全稀
釋後 EPS 分別為 75.25、134.07 及 178.24 元。考量 AI 供應鏈遇短期逆風，影響
評價表現，研究部將本益比調整為 18 倍，維持買進建議，目標價 2,810 元(2027
年及 2028 年之平均預估 EPS 156.16*18XPER)。

ESG 評析 
貿聯企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

### Page 2

2

貿聯-KY 是全球領先的連接器公司之一：貿聯-KY 是全球領先的連接器公司之一：
貿聯-KY 總部設於加州 Fremont，3Q26 產品比重為資訊科技與數據傳輸 48％、
工業用 31％、車用 8％、電器 12％；若以事業別區分，HPC 占 46％、半導體設
備(SPE)占 13％、其他事業占 41％。貿聯主要核心能力，在於高技術及客製化開
發零組件製造，產品開發以多角化及差異化策略，2026/1 完成收購新富生光電
(XFS)，2026/9/23 完成收購 Interplex 集團資料通訊業務(Interplex Datacom)，
產品線由電源與數據連接延伸至光纖跳線、機殼及機櫃等精密機構件，朝 AI 機櫃
次系統解決方案供應商發展。 
3Q26 財報公布，優於市場預期，營收與獲利同創新高，營益率首度突破 20%：
3Q26 營收 278.75 億元(+19.7% QoQ、+51.4% YoY)，毛利率 32.53%，季增 1.8
個百分點；營業利益率 20.78%，季增 3.6 個百分點、年增 1.2 個百分點，為公司
首度突破 20%，獲利表現優於市場預期。成長主要來自 HPC，3Q26 HPC 營收季
增 30%、年增 61%，營收比重由 2Q26 的 42%回升至 46%，其中電源產品為成
長主力，帶動產品組合改善；半導體設備營收亦季增 11%、年增 70%，各事業全
面成長。費用方面，營收規模擴大但營業費用未等比例增加，營業費用率降至約
12%的歷史低點，顯示獲利率改善來自規模效益與費用效率，而非一次性因素，具
備延續性。稅後淨利 42.93 億元(+44.0% QoQ、+63.3% YoY)，稀釋後 EPS 21.51
元，營收、淨利與 EPS 皆創歷史新高；前三季累計 EPS 48.04 元，已超越 2025 全
年之 46.07 元。

### Page 3

3

研究部上修 2026~2028 年營收獲利預估，原因有三： 
1. Interplex 資料通訊業務(Datacom Business)營收提前兩年達標：Interplex
的資料通訊業務(Datacom Business)自 9/23 起併表，3Q26 僅貢獻 7 天營
收；10 月為首個完整併表月，帶動貿聯 10 月營收達 118.46 億元，月增 26.9%、
年增 80.0%，其中 Interplex 貢獻 6,700 萬美元(約新台幣 21 億元)。對照公司
宣布併購時揭露之年營收 3.92 億美元(月平均約 3,300 萬美元)，Interplex 月
營收已倍增，大幅優於市場預期。研究部看好併購綜效將逐步顯現：Interplex
主要供應 AI 伺服器機殼、機櫃等精密機構件，研究部認為其營收較併購時大
幅成長，主因 AI 機櫃出貨量較年初明顯放大，且併入貿聯後，Interplex 可運
用集團的資金、客戶關係與製造資源擴大接單，雙方產品整合後可提供涵蓋配
電、數據連接、機構件與液冷的機櫃次系統方案，單一 AI 機櫃之可供應產值
明顯擴大。研究部本次上修 2027、2028 年 Interplex Datacom Business 為
貿聯帶來的營收預估，分別為 268、308 億元(原預估 183、250 億元)。 
2. 新富生光通訊規模快速放大，營收占比持續提升：新富生(XFS) 專精於光纖互
連產品的設計與製造，主要產品包括單芯／雙芯跳線、光纖尾纖、多芯光纖以
及 MTP/MPO 高密度連接器，月營收規模自 2026 年初以來成長逾一倍，獲
利能力同步改善，展現規模化高精密光學互連業務的效益。資料中心對光纖組
件的品質與一致性要求遠高於一般應用，新富生在高階光纖跳線的製造經驗與
品質優勢，加上貿聯集團的客戶網絡與全球產能布局，有助其持續拓展新客戶。
隨 AI 資料中心機櫃間光纖連接需求成長，研究部預估新富生 2027 年營收可
望再翻倍增長，且其獲利率改善亦有助提升貿聯整體獲利結構。 
3. 3Q26 營益率創新高，營運槓桿持續發揮：3Q26 營業費用率約 12%，較 1H26
的 13.7%明顯下降，顯示貿聯在承接更大規模、更複雜的客戶專案時，製造、
工程與行政職能的效率同步提升，費用無須等比例增加。4Q26 起 Interplex
完整併表，營收規模再擴大，研究部預期營益率改善幅度可望大於毛利率，稅
後淨利成長幅度將高於營收。 
HPC 電源成長最強，新平台升級為下一波動能：3Q26 HPC 營收年增 61%、季增
30%，前三季累計年增 77%。隨運算功耗提升，電源產品切入規格更高的新一代
平台；數據連接方面，貿聯同時布局銅纜與光纖，因應不同傳輸距離與頻寬需求。
貿聯於 10 月 OCP Global Summit 2026 展出適用於 OCP 及 NVIDIA MGX 機櫃
之 800V/400VDC 高功率電源方案，以及 1.6T 液冷交換器 OSFP 連接器、機櫃管
理控制與漏液偵測系統，產品範圍由電源線材延伸至配電、液冷與監控。研究部認
為，隨 Vera Rubin 平台放量及 HVDC 架構導入，單一機櫃的電源產品規格與產值
將持續提升，為 2027 年 HPC 成長主軸。 
次世代光通訊布局：貿聯 2026 年先後於 OFC 展示 Optical Shuffle、於 Computex
展示 CPO 交換器托盤方案；9 月於 ECOC 2026 展示與 ams OSRAM 合作之
microVCSEL 光學互連方案，採用 850nm 二維可定址 microVCSEL 陣列，以多通
道、低速率(wide-and-slow)架構降低功耗與延遲，貿聯負責光纖陣列組裝、光學
封裝、光學測試與量產製造；10 月於 OCP Global Summit 2026 展出 CPO、
800G/1.6T 及 XPO 光收發模組、光纖陣列(FAU)與多芯光纖連接等方案。公司表
示產業未來可能並行採用多種光學架構，貿聯以製造與整合能力參與各技術路線，
不需押注單一方案。 
半導體設備成長幅度為產業兩倍以上：3Q26 半導體設備(SPE)營收年增 70%、季
增 11%，營收占比由 3Q25 的 11%提升至 13%，前三季累計年增 48%。根據國
際半導體產業協會（SEMI）於 2026 年 7 月公布之預測，全球前段晶圓製造設備

### Page 4

4

(WFE)市場 2026 年預估年增 23.1%至 1,439 億美元，貿聯 SPE 成長幅度約為產業
的兩倍；自 2023 年以來貿聯之 SPE 業務成長即大幅超越整體 WFE 市場，超額成
長主因貿聯在客戶端的角色由供應線材零組件，擴大至高階組裝、模組與次系統製
造、測試及系統整合，應用以薄膜沉積與蝕刻設備為主，微影與檢測設備為輔；隨
設備製程日益複雜，客戶傾向將更多製造工作委外，以集中資源於核心技術，貿聯
單一設備平台之內含價值隨之提升。SEMI 預估 WFE 市場 2027 年及 2028 年將分
別成長 21.8%及 14.1%，2028 年規模可望突破 2,000 億美元，主要由先進邏輯、
HBM 等先進記憶體投資帶動；公司亦表示，許多客戶對設備投資的強勁預期延伸
至 2030 年以後，且客戶正主動協助貿聯擴充產能，以支援其自身長期成長。貿聯
在 SPE 領域的投資以擴充製造產能為主，Interplex 的精密機械加工與次系統整合
能力亦可與 SPE 業務互補，為 HPC 以外的第二成長引擎。 
隨伺服器架構升級驅動產值倍增，2027~2028 年獲利成長路徑清晰，重申買進： 
研究部認為，貿聯本次財報的關鍵不只是單季獲利優於預期，而是兩項併購同時優
於預期，帶動營收基期結構性墊高：10 月營收 118.46 億元，較 3Q26 月平均約
93 億元高出約 27%。在 AI 機櫃同時需要電源、數據傳輸(銅纜與光纖)與精密機構
件的趨勢下，貿聯透過 Interplex 與新富生補齊產品版圖，單一機櫃可供應產值持
續擴大；而未來一年內，Interplex 完整併表(4Q26)、新富生營收比重提升(2027)、
Vera Rubin 平台與 HVDC 電 源 放 量 (2027~2028) 及 次 世 代 光 互 連 產 品
(2H27~2028)等成長催化劑將陸續到位，獲利上修動能明確。 
評價方面，短期因市場擔憂 GPU 產品推出節奏可能放緩，台灣 AI 供應鏈評價短
期受到壓抑。研究部認為，CSP 資本支出與現行平台訂單短期不受影響，且貿聯的
客戶與產品布局相對分散：公司採取平台中立策略，產品同時供應 GPU 與 ASIC
架構之 AI 機櫃，並服務多家雲端服務業者；銅纜與光纖、電源與機構件等產品線
亦不依賴單一技術路線；此外，半導體設備與其他事業合計仍占營收逾五成，營運
受單一平台影響的程度低於純 GPU 供應鏈。惟考量主要客戶經營層異動可能影響
新平台推出節奏，研究部於評價上給予折價，本益比由前次之 25 倍調整為 18 倍。
搭配本次上修後之 EPS，維持買進建議，目標價 2,810 元(2027 年及 2028 年之平
均預估 EPS 156.16*18XPER)。

## Document 296: AI基建股蒸发4.2万亿美元，“卖铲盛宴”终结？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI基建股蒸发4.2万亿美元，“卖铲盛宴”终结？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

6 ⽉以来，卡特彼勒跌 44% 、 Oklo 暴跌 49% 。分析师直指根源：投资者将传统⼯业股当成
⻓股炒作，估值严重虚⾼。此次抛售的另⼀重要背景，是美国中期选举后数据中⼼监管政
策⾛向的不明朗。基本⾯仍具韧性 —— 卡特彼勒积压订单达 780 亿美元，多家企业营收增⻓
已锁定⾄ 2027 年。
摘要：
全球制造、公⽤事业与矿业企业⾃今年 6 ⽉以来市值合计蒸发 4.2 万亿美元，这场抛售⼏乎抹去了
此前 12 个⽉内同类企业所积累的 9.4 万亿美元涨幅的近半数，曾⻛靡⼀时的 AI“ 卖铲 ” 交易正在急
速逆转。
据英国《⾦融时报》分析，在媒体报道中与数据中⼼建设关联度越⾼的企业，遭受的冲击越为
惨烈。投资者对 AI 热潮的信⼼正在动摇，从适配超⼤规模数据中⼼的燃⽓发电机组制造商，到连
接 AI 服务器机架所需的⾼端光纤企业，此前被争相追捧的标的如今纷纷遭到抛售。⼯业板块已成
为今夏以来标普 500 指数中表现第⼆差的板块，仅次于科技板块。
市场的转向折射出更⼴泛的担忧：超⼤规模云计算企业持续以债务融资推⾼资本开⽀，叠加美

国国内对数据中⼼建设⽇益⾼涨的政治阻⼒，令投资者开始质疑这场建设热潮能否持续。
⻰头企业市值腰斩
数据中⼼燃⽓发电机组的主要供应商卡特彼勒（ Caterpillar ）过去六个⽉跌幅超过 30% ，⽽⾃ 6
⽉ 30 ⽇股价触及历史⾼点以来，市值已缩⽔ 2140 亿美元，跌幅达 44% 。
此外，核电企业 Oklo 尽管在今年 1 ⽉与 Meta 签署了供电合同，股价仍重挫 49% 。暖通空调系统制
造商 Comfort Systems 与光纤制造商康宁（ Corning ）在同⼀时期均损失了约⼀半市值。
据英国⾦融时报称，德意志银⾏⾼级美国经济学家 Brett Ryan 表⽰，此前有⼤量机构资⾦ “ 盲⽬
涌⼊ ” 这些交易， “ 可能助推了部分企业过于乐观的估值 ”。他还指出，股票市场正在 “ 向信贷市
场早已发出的信号靠拢 ”—— 后者对数据中⼼建设中⻛险融资的增⻓早有警⽰。
⻛险投资⼈、⿇省理⼯学院研究员 Paul Kedrosky 则直⾔，投资者将传统⼯业企业当作半导体股
票来对待，本⾝就是⼀个错误。

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

不过，尽管股价承压，多家企业的经营基本⾯依然稳健。美国政府最新数据显⽰，截⾄今年 9 ⽉
的 12 个⽉内，美国数据中⼼建设⽀出（不含 IT 设备与芯⽚）达到 960 亿美元。
第三季度业绩同样印证了需求的韧性。卡特彼勒报告积压订单达创纪录的 780 亿美元，其发电业
务销售额同⽐增⻓ 75% ，主要由数据中⼼需求驱动。西⻔⼦来⾃数据中⼼的电⽓设备订单积压
在 2026 年增⻓ 60% ，达到 70 亿欧元；伊顿（ Eaton ）同类订单则实现同⽐翻倍。多家燃⽓轮机
制造商表⽰， 2026 年前九个⽉已斩获数⼗亿美元订单。
Dell'Oro Group 分析师 Alex Cordovil 表⽰， “ 就这些企业的营收基本⾯和潜在盈利前景⽽⾔，我
们仍然看到⾮常强劲的预期。 ” 他补充道，电⽓化和供应链重构等更宏观的趋势持续⽀撑着众多⼯
业企业的核⼼业务， “ 建设并未减速 …… 积压订单处于历史⾼位，这意味着⾄少到 2027 年，营收
增⻓已基本锁定。 ”
⼯业控制设备制造商 Allient ⾸席执⾏官 Richard S Warzala 在 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇的业绩电话会上表⽰，对 AI

的需求不会下滑。
此外， Kedrosky 对订单积压数据持保留态度，认为这是滞后指标，反映的是此前的预期，⽽⾮
当下的真实需求。他指出，企业存在 " 双重乃⾄三重下单 " 的现象 —— 为了在感知到的供应紧缺中
抢占排队位置⽽超额备货。
Brett Ryan 的判断则更为直接：股票市场正在完成⼀次迟来的估值校正，向信贷市场早已传递的
⻛险信号对⻬。这场历时数⽉、市值蒸发逾 4 万亿美元的调整，本质上是资本市场对 " ⼀切皆成⻓
股 " 叙事的系统性重新定价。
中期选举后的监管不确定性
此次抛售的另⼀重要背景，是美国中期选举后数据中⼼监管政策⾛向的不明朗。在本⽉的中期
选举中，对数据中⼼建设实施限制的提案获得了美国选⺠的⼴泛⽀持，但其实际影响⽬前仍不
明朗。
花旗研究在选举后发布的报告中指出，尽管预计更严格的监管将给数据中⼼⾏业带来更多波
动，但⼤范围限制建设的可能性仍然较低。
星座能源（ Constellation Energy ）⾸席执⾏官 Joseph Dominguez 表⽰，选举后的市场反应是

“ 过度反应 ” ，并强调⺠主党⼈ “ ⾮常在意 ” 保护数据中⼼的蓝领就业岗位。
德意志银⾏的 Ryan 则认为，即便部分地区出台禁令，也只会将建设需求转移⾄监管更为宽松的
地区。
“ 在与对冲基⾦和家族办公室的讨论中， ‘ ⼀切都是成⻓股 ’ 这种论调⽆处不在 —— 这当然是荒谬
的。 ”
“ 即便对新数据中⼼的建设实施暂停令，那些已经开⼯的项⽬也必须完成。 ”
“ 归根结底，如果市场对这些设施存在需求，它们终将在某个地⽅建成。 ”

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 340: daily story_UAA Under Armour_劉昃恩_20261109

Source PDF: all report pdf/daily story_UAA Under Armour_劉昃恩_20261109.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 運動用品 ‧美國

Under Armour 
(UAA.O/UAA US)

客流量下滑考驗 
重要訊息 
Under Armour 2Q FY27 (7-9 月；財年止於 3 月) 營運約當損平。儘管 FY27
營收年減中個位數之展望不變，管理層下調毛利率指引，反映考量油價、
運價和庫存清倉等衝擊迎面而來。 
評論及分析 
2Q FY27 營運保持損平。2Q FY27 每股盈餘 0.00 美元，符合預期。營收
12.4 億美元，年減幅度擴大至 7%，反映全產品/通路需求皆轉差。地區別
方面，北美、歐非中東及亞太平洋銷售分別年減 7%、9%、9%，僅拉美
為唯一成長市場，年增 3%。再加上通路、地區組合不利與折扣力道加大，
毛利率延續衰退走勢，年減 0.3、季減 7.1 個百分點至 47.0%，拖累營利率
至僅 1.2%，年減 0.1、季減 3.1 個百分點。 
產業困境。管理層觀察到歐美客流量下滑與同業促銷力道加大風險，預計
3Q FY27 營收將年減 8-9% (連三季惡化)，劣於市場共識之 4%衰退；其中，
北美及歐非中東將為風暴中心，分別年減低雙位數、高個位數，亞太則將
改善至約年持平。加上油價和運價上漲、庫存清倉與匯率不利等因素疊加
影響，FY27 毛利率指引下調至 47.2-47.2% (前次為 47.7-48.2%)，且年增
1.7-2.2 個百分點中約有 1.5 個百分點係來自美國退稅挹注。 
庫存去化策略。2Q FY27 庫存週轉天數年增 7%、但季減 17%至 149 天，
仍高於過去十年同期平均水準之 131 天，主因營運規模縮減凸顯在途存貨
較高。管理層表示正與合作工廠協商推遲部分發貨，同時考慮 2H FY27 從
批發端去化庫存，但將避開同業清倉甩賣的場所。公司計畫持續減少產品
SKU、聚焦高端或細分領域 (例如美式足球釘鞋已成為美國第二大品牌)，
同時簡化工作流程，確保長期利潤率改善空間。 
投資建議 
考量歐美客流量逆風與促銷影響仍延續，我們維持 Under Armour「持有」
評等，目標價改採 FY28 每股淨值 1.6 倍推算，調整為 5.5 美元。 
投資風險 
上檔風險為消費較預期強勁與品牌重拾人氣。

### Page 2

美國 Under Armour

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Under Armour 11 月 6 日法說會重點摘要 
 2Q FY27 (財年止於 3 月；7-9 月) 每股盈餘為 0.00 美元，符合預期。
營收 12.4 億美元，年減幅度擴大至 7%，反映全產品及全通路需求皆
轉差。受通路、地區組合不利與折扣力道加大影響，毛利率年減 0.3、
季減 7.1 個百分點至 47.0%，拖累營利率至僅 1.2%。 
 產品別營收：服飾 (佔總營收 71%)、鞋類 (19%) 及配件 (8%) 全面
惡化至年減 6%、11%、7%。 
 市場別營收：北美 (佔總營收 59%)、歐非中東 (23%) 及亞太平洋 
(13%) 分別年減 7%、9%、9%，符合公司指引。拉美 (4%) 年增
3%，為唯一成長市場。 
 通路別營收：批發業務 (佔總營收 58%) 年減 6%，再度轉弱，主因
全價商品銷售疲軟及部分分銷管道遇逆風。直接面對消費者 (DTC，
40%) 衰退擴大至 8%，反映客流量壓力導致直營店與電商業務分別
年減 7%、12%。 
 2Q FY27 庫存週轉天數年增 7%、但季減 17%至 149 天，仍高於過去
十年同期平均水準之 131 天，主因在途存貨較高。公司正透過與合作
工廠協商推遲部分發貨，同時考慮 2H FY27 從批發端去化庫存，但將
避開同業清倉甩賣的場所。 
 FY27 營收維持年減中個位數之展望，但基於油價和運價上漲、庫存
清倉與匯率不利等因素影響，將毛利率指引下調至 47.2-47.2% (前次
為 47.7-48.2%)，且年增 1.7-2.2 個百分點中約有 1.5 個百分點係來自
美國退稅挹注。加上組織轉型與重組費用認列，預計每股虧損 0.02
至每股盈餘 0.02 美元。 
 短期而言，管理層觀察到歐美客流量下滑與同業促銷力道加大風險，
預計 3Q FY27 營收將年減 8-9%；其中，北美及歐非中東將分別年減
低雙位數、高個位數，亞太約呈年持平。加上毛利率年增 0.3-0.5 個
百分點展望，3Q FY27 營運落後市場預期。

圖 6: 公司概況  圖 7: 服飾貢獻營收最大比重 
成立於 1996 年，Under Armour 為國際領導運動品牌，致力
提供專業運動服飾、鞋類及配件；其產品採用創新材料技術，
包括吸濕排汗、溫度控管、緩衝與布料永續利用等，目的在
創造差異化，並提升消費者品牌體驗。美洲為 FY26 營收主要
來源地區，佔比達 58%，其次分別為歐非中東 24%、亞太平
洋 14%、拉丁美洲 4%。

股價說明 等級 定義 
 增加持股 (OP) 對個股持正面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現超越凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總
報酬。 
 持有 (N) 對個股持中性看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現符合凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總
報酬。 
 降低持股 (U) 對個股持負面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現低於凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總
報酬。 
 未評等 (NR) 凱基證券未對該個股加以評等。 
 受法規限制 
未評等 (R) 
受凱基證券集團內部政策和/或相關法令限制使凱基證券集團無法進行某些形式的資訊交
流，其中包括提供評等給投資人參考。 
  *總報酬 = (十二個月目標價-現價)/現價 
免責聲明

ECONOMIC RESEARCH
Change since m = 0 = February 2026 / February 2022.
Unit: inflation change in percentage point, point for confidence surveys
September 2026: Inflation accelerated again

but only slightly  …
The
average
CPI
inflation
rate
of
the
fifteen
leading
emerging
economies
rose
again
year
-
on
-
year
in
September
(
4
.
6
%
compared
with
4
.
3
%
in
August)
;
a
similar
acceleration
had
already
been
observed
in
the
Spring
.
However,
the
inflationary
impact
of
the
conflict
remains
lower
than
in
2022
.
Manufacturers’
views
on
trends
in
the
prices
of
inputs
and
finished
goods
have
returned
to
their
pre
-
conflict
levels
.
However,

agricultural
and
food
prices
have
been
rising
more
rapidly
in
recent
months
against
a
backdrop
of
increasingly
frequent
and
destructive
extreme
weather
events
.
The
manufacturing
PMI
average
index
of
the
fifteen
leading
emerging
economies
has
almost
returned
to
its
February
2026
level
since
September
.
The
main
countries
for
which
the
overall
index
remains
well
below
that
level
are
Brazil,
Chile
and
Türkiye
.
Despite
the
AI
boom,
the
overall
indices

and/or
opinion
of
export
order
books
in
China,
South
Korea
and
Taiwan
are
not
significantly
higher
than
in
February
.
For
these
countries,
the
recovery
is
therefore,
in
principle,
less
pronounced
in
manufacturing
sectors
that
do
not
benefit
directly
from
the
AI
boom
.
According
to
estimates
by
the
Institute
for
International
Finance,
non
-
resident
portfolio
investment
flows
into
the
main
emerging
markets
have
been
particularly
volatile
since
the

start
of
the
armed
conflict,
reflecting
investor
jitters
.
Looking
at
cumulative
figures
since
February
provides
a
clearer
picture
of
the
trends
.
In
the
equity
markets,
outflows
have
been
much
greater
than
in
2022
(particularly
from
South
Korea
and
Taiwan)
.
They
resumed
in
September
and
October
after
stabilising
over
the
summer
.
In
the
bond
markets,
unlike
in
2022
,
net
flows
remained
positive
until
July
and
have
been
stable
since
then
.
Except

for
a
very
few
cases,
domestic
interest
rates
and
risk
premiums
have
held
up
very
well
against
the
shock
(much
better
than
in
2022
),
as
well
as
against
the
tensions
in
the
bond
markets
of
advanced
economies,
which
intensified
this
summer
.

► 美股自上週的低點反彈，上週跌幅最深的費
半反彈幅度最大，反映短線市場恐慌情緒有
所緩解。然而，AI疑慮並未完全消散，儘管
德州州長當選人Gina Hinojosa表示並非全面
反對資料中心開發，但資料中心監管趨嚴的
疑慮仍在市場迴盪。另一方面，10月CPI數
據高於預期，搭配優於預期的非農數據，支
撐了市場對於聯準會12月升息1碼之預估，

加上輝達執行長黃仁勳的相關事件，皆成為
短線股市反彈能否延續所需觀察的變數。
► 產業方面，CoreWeave財報與展望優於市場
預期，並進一步說明積壓訂單、客戶承諾與
融資規畫，帶動半導體、伺服器、記憶體及
資料中心相關個股買盤。然而，輝達則受執
行長 黃 仁 勳遭 到 調 查且 宣 布 請辭 之 消 息影
響， 股 價 因而 回 落 並抵 銷 科 技類 股 部 分漲
勢。其餘產業則是呈現上漲，但醫療保健、
必需消費等防禦型產業在恐慌情緒放緩下多
數漲幅落後於成長型產業。

態度反覆變化令市場對美伊談判疑慮再起，
預料油價短期將呈現區間震盪。
► 受市場強化聯準會升息預期支撐，帶動美元
指數呈現強勁上漲，並同步拖累歐元與日圓
顯著走弱，其中美元兌日圓匯價已逼近160
之整數關卡，短期恐引發干預措施的預期，
走勢料偏向震盪。

美國CPI 美國核心CPI
月增率，%
► 受能源價格增速放緩影響，美國10月CPI月
增率由前值0.6%降至0.4%，核心CPI月增率
維持0.3%水準，惟整體表現高於預期，其中
食品、住房及運輸服務持續上漲，顯示核心
通膨相 對具 有黏 性 ，物價 壓力未 見全 面緩
解；此外，因去年聯邦政府關門造成統計缺
漏，故本次不公布年增率數值。考量美國就
業市場表現強勁，通膨降溫進程不如預期，
疊加近期聯準會官員偏鷹發言，強化市場押
注年底升息一碼之預期。
► 隨民主黨拿下參眾兩院，美伊重啟和談訊號
浮現，市場對戰事降溫抱持正向期待，帶動
國際油價一度自高檔回落；此外，受2026年
油價高基期影響，若油價未再進一步出現大
幅攀升，預估2027年原油價格年增速可望趨

於收斂，對於整體通膨的推升力道亦有望減
緩。美國方面，受惠於頁岩油生產效率提升
及墨西哥灣新離岸油田陸續投產，則可望帶
動美國原油產量持續成長。
熱門議題
熱門議題
通膨水準仍高，鞏固年底升息預期能源價格增速趨緩，惟核心物價壓力未除
美國CPI數據
美國通膨降溫不如預期，關注原油供需變化與升息路徑

### Page 6

6
投資焦點
► 近期美股市場風格加速轉換，隨著市場更加
重視AI商業化成效與投資回報，高估值成長
股波動顯著升溫。相較之下，價值股則逐漸
受到市場青睞，尤其是憑藉穩定現金流、健
康資產負債表與較高股東回報率之高品質價
值股，除了擁有價值優勢之外，更具備更好
防禦力與抗震能力。
► 從風險維度進行觀察，近10年股市面臨3次
升息循環，不論是2016年、2022年甚至是
2026年9月啟動之升息，高品質價值股指數
之90天波動度平均值皆低於成長股與大盤指
數，顯示若將具備穩定獲利、自由現金流與
較低財 務槓 桿之 高 品質價 值股納 入投 資組
合，有助降低市場修正期間的波動風險。
► 長期而言，高品質價值股的累積報酬率雖不
及成長股在景氣擴張期的爆發力，但仍具優
於大盤的累積報酬潛力，代表在市場波動加
劇、利率升高的環境下，其兼具防禦與長期
複利之優勢已然是長期投資不可或缺關鍵。
高品質價值股長期報酬優於大盤價值股相對成長股獲市場青睞，波動度亦相對偏低
成長股短線波動大，留意價值股表現機會

### Page 7

7
投資焦點國防股
► 國防股通常具備穩定攀升之自由現金流，主
要受惠於長期政府合約與產業高進入門檻所
支持。相較景氣循環股，國防產業獲利與現
金流波動幅度較低，使其兼具一定程度的防
禦性與長期價值配置優勢。
► 北美國防企業近年受惠於地緣政治風險升溫
與軍備補庫存需求，訂單能見度明顯提升。
包含飛彈、防空系統、軍機及潛艦等關鍵裝
備，有助維持歷史高檔積壓訂單水位。龐大
訂單庫存不僅支撐未來數年營收成長，也有
助降低短期景氣波動對企業基本面的影響。
► 根據SIPRI 資料，2025 年 全 球 軍 事 支 出 達
2.89兆美元，已連續11年成長，全球軍事支
出占GDP比重升至2.5%，創2009年以來新
高。其中歐洲軍費年增14%、亞洲與大洋洲
年增 8.1%。在美伊衝突、區域安全風險及北
約提高國防支出目標等因素帶動下，各國軍
事現代化需求可望延續，皆將為國防產業提
供長期且具結構性的成長動能。
地緣政治問題未解，全球軍事支出持續增加國防產業現金流穩定攀升，積壓訂單成長強勁
現金流穩定，補庫存預期支撐後續國防股走勢

### Page 8

8
股市
 黃仁勳相關事件恐逐步強化美國對AI晶片出口管制、AI資料中心建設進程或將受到州政府緊縮政策
而造成評價面壓力並增添股價波動。短期可留意防禦股以增強抗震力以抵禦市場震盪。長期而言，
AI從全面看好轉向選股，且大優於小，市場波瀾過後產業長期正向趨勢未變。當前則可布局財務健
全度較高的高品質價值股，以獲取穩定報酬並降低市場波動影響。
 歐、日股可分散配置，利率走升有利銀行股。此外，隨財政政策支持，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好轉向選股、且大優於小，市場波
瀾後長期正向趨勢未變，當前可布局財務
健全度較高的高品質價值股
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 美伊談判走向反覆未明，油價維持高檔震盪。10月CPI高於預期，強化市場對聯準會升息步伐預估，
聯準會官員立場亦偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率
走升時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具
吸引力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。

債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 10月CPI數據支撐升息預期，美元走勢偏強。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。
美元高檔整理
商品
 美伊談判走向反覆，油價一度回落但再度反彈，短線仍偏震盪。
 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
低接買盤流入，黃金低點反彈，中長期維
持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 短期AI相關變數持續發酵，高品質價值股有望獲得青睞

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 441: Tracking Europe’s AI transition_ A new STOXX 600 earnings-call framework (2026-11-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Tracking Europe’s AI transition_ A new STOXX 600 earnings-call framework (2026-11-11).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

Tracking Europe’s AI transition:  
A new STOXX 600 earnings-call 
framework

1 
Authors 
Michael Kirker 
Economist

November 11, 2026 
How quickly is AI moving from experimentation into everyday business, and 
where are companies reporting tangible gains? The answers matter for 
institutional investors and policymakers assessing productivity, operating 
efficiency and labour demand. Yet comparable evidence across Europe remains 
limited, making it difficult to track the pace and scale of the transition. 1 
We address this gap with a proprietary framework that turns what is said on 
STOXX 600 earnings call into three structured indicators: AI adoption 
intensity, reported productivity benefits, and the impact on workforces. 
Large language models analyse what management teams say about AI’s 
operational role, applying a common scoring framework across companies, 
sectors and countries. Our new approach provides a timely pan-European 
comparable tracker that captures not just whether AI is being mentioned, but its 
real-world impact as the adoption of the new technology unfolds. 
Key Takeaways: 
— Adoption is broadening, with signs of faster progress. AI-use scores rose 
gradually from a low base before strengthening over the past 12 months. The 
increase extends across sectors, although the pace and level of adoption 
remain uneven. 
— Workforce impacts remain limited in corporate disclosures. The 
framework does not show a broad negative workforce signal attributable to 
AI. The aggregate score remains close to the level of no impact on the labour 
force across all of our sample. However, this does not rule out AI displacing 
workers at a future date. 
— Geographic differences persist within the listed-company sample. Since 
2022, German companies in the STOXX 600 have generally recorded above-
average AI-use and reported productivity scores. Dutch and Spanish 
companies in the STOXX 600 have tended to score lower on AI use, although 
Spain has recently gone through a rapid catch up. 
— The technology J-Curve: The findings are consistent with an early stage of 
the AI transition. Companies report increasing adoption of the new 
technology, but transformational productivity gains and extensive workforce 
restructuring remain uncommon.

1 1 Our US team regularly publish on data from the USCB showing the impact of 
AI on businesses, and our German team have recently begun their own 
chartbook. In the UK, the Office for National Statistics publishes on the topic too.

### Page 2

Deutsche Bank AG Page 2

Methodology 
Sample period: Our sample begins in January 2022, providing a baseline before 
ChatGPT’s launch on 30 November 2022. AI in this framework covers both 
established and emerging technologies, so the launch of ChatGPT is a reference 
point rather than the start of all corporate AI adoption. 
Timing of observations: STOXX 600 companies report on different financial 
calendars and at quarterly or semi-annual intervals. Their earnings calls also mix 
discussion of completed activity with forward-looking guidance. 
We assign each transcript to the calendar quarter in which the earnings call takes 
place, regardless of the financial reporting period discussed. The resulting 
indicators therefore track when information about AI use and its operational 
impacts becomes public rather than when implementation or the reported effect 
occurred. This provides a common dating convention across the sample while 
retaining differences in reporting frequency and disclosure timing.

Figure 1: Coverage of the STOXX 600 earnings calls

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: Share of the STOXX 600 is based on the current company weight in the index. Data collection for the current quarter (Q4 2026) is incomplete 
as some companies are still to report

Constructing the STOXX 600 AI corpus: We use a specialised AI transcription 
engine to process earnings calls from STOXX 600 companies, restricting the 
analysis to calls for which we obtain complete transcripts of sufficient accuracy. 
In Q3 2026, the latest full quarter, the dataset covered more than 500 companies, 
representing approximately 90% of the index’s current market capitalisation 
weights (Figure 1). In the current quarter (Q4) earnings calls are still taking place. 
Nearly 250 companies with an equivalent weight to around c.60% of the STOXX 
index having already reported. 
Historical coverage typically ranges from 300 to 500 companies and represents 
more than 80% of the index’s current market-capitalisation weights. Coverage 
peaks twice a year because some European companies report semi-annually.  
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 3

Deutsche Bank AG Page 3

### Page 4

Deutsche Bank AG Page 4

For examples of the AI’s scoring and justifications, see the tables presented in the 
Annex at the end of this report. 
Summary statistics 
Figure 2 shows the distribution of scores across all earnings-call transcripts in the 
historical sample, without weighting for company size or reporting frequency. 
These are transcript-level distributions rather than shares of companies, and they 
combine early and recent observations. They provide context for the quarterly 
indicators: 
• AI use score: Approximately 60% of earnings call transcripts do not 
discuss AI use. Among the minority that do discuss it, most fall into the 
“Active implementation” range (a score of 4-6 out of 10). 
• AI’s productivity impact: Nearly 70% of earnings calls do not mention a 
tangible AI-related productivity benefit. Among calls that do, scores are 
generally in the medium-impact band, indicating notable operational 
improvements. Transformational gains, scored at 8 or above out of 10, 
are rarely reported. 
• AI’s labour impact: Only around 10% of earnings calls discuss AI-related 
workforce adjustments. Reported changes typically receive scores of 1–
2 in magnitude on the −5 to +5 scale, with a slight tilt towards hiring and 
upskilling rather than redundancies.

Figure 2: Raw distribution of scores across the entire sample

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Note: Results are based on the raw number of transcripts, and are independent of firm size, and biased towards firms reporting more often.

Taken together, the distributions are consistent with an early adoption phase: 
companies discuss AI implementation more often than transformational 
productivity gains or extensive workforce restructuring. A technology J-curve 
offers one interpretation of this pattern, with investment and organisational 
adaptation preceding broader benefits.  
This J-curve pattern is strongly corroborated by external micro-data, such as the 
Banco de España’s business activity survey (EBAE) of Spanish firms, which 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 5

Deutsche Bank AG Page 5

similarly indicates that AI has a more immediate impact on upfront investment 
and productivity than on headcount. 
AI use: A broad-based rise, with signs of faster recent adoption 
The new AI-use indicator points to a broadening transition across STOXX 600 
companies, with early signs that adoption has accelerated recently. Scores rose 
only gradually from 2022 and 2024, but the latest observations suggest a 
stronger pace across industries and country groups.  
The rise is broad-based rather than confined to one sector. Technology has led 
much of the sample, while financials show tentative recent outperformance and 
utilities have caught up from a low starting point. Among the large country groups, 
German companies have persistently recorded above-average scores.  
AI use across European industries 
To trace sectoral dispersion, we group companies by STOXX industry 
classification and calculate a weighted average of their scores for each calendar 
quarter, using their latest STOXX 600 index weights. 2  
We focus on four sectors spanning different business models: financials, the 
largest services sector; industrials, the largest physical-assets sector; technology, 
a likely early adopter; and utilities, an infrastructure-heavy sector where adoption 
may proceed more slowly.  
Figure 3 presents the AI use score for these four key industries alongside the 
range for all industry scores (represented by the shaded area). 
• The AI use score is generally low over time but is gradually rising from 
around 1 point in 2022 to around 3 in 2026. 
• AI-use scores are rising across industries, but differences between 
sectors are widening. The common direction therefore points to 
broadening adoption rather than uniform progress: sectors can 
participate in the transition while remaining at different levels of use. 
In terms of the industries of interest: 
• Technology’s early lead has narrowed. The sector’s AI-use score was 
near the top of the industry range through to 2024 but has recently 
plateaued as the broader STOXX 600 average caught up during 2025–
26.3 
• Financials and industrials broadly track the index average. Both sectors 
have remained close to the aggregate AI-use score over history, with 
financials generally a little below average, and industrials a little above. 
But financials show tentative signs of moving above average in recent 
quarters.

2 The industries are: (1) Basic Materials, (2), Consumer Discretionary, (3), Consumer Staples, (4) Energy, (5) 
Financials, (6) Health Care, (7) Industrials, (9) Real Estate, (10) Technology, (11) Telecommunications, (12) 
Utilities. 
3 Q4 2025 saw a large increase in the AI use score for the “Technology” industry, jumping up to around 6. This 
one-off spike was driven by ASML’s announcement of a significant strategic partnership with and investment 
in Mistral AI” to 'embed AI across our entire holistic portfolio in order to increase the performance and 
productivity of our systems and the yield of our customers' processes'. Because of ASML’s large market cap 
(around 49% of our Technology category), it distorts the weighted average significantly. 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 6

Deutsche Bank AG Page 6

• Utilities have caught up from a low base. After recording lower AI-use 
scores than other sectors for much of the sample, utilities converged 
towards the STOXX 600 average during 2026. The result shows that the 
rise in reported adoption extends beyond the sectors most closely 
associated with AI.

Figure 3: AI use score (0-10) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The value for each industry is computed as a weighted average of all firms within the industry where the weights are derived from the current 
STOXX 600 share of each company. The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

As a cross-check, our findings align with the European Commission’s October 
Eurobarometer Flash survey of MSEs and Mid-Caps and the Eurostat annual ICT 
surveys, which show low overall intensity but a similar expansion in service- and 
tech-related sectors.  
AI use across countries 
Companies in the STOXX 600 tend to be large multi-nationals. That makes it 
difficult to pin down where physically the AI transformation is taking place within 
their organisation. Instead, our country comparison is done using two 
classifications of a company’s country: 
• Country of domicile: AI is a transformation technology, and this type of 
change is likely to be driven in organisation from the top down. We 
therefore consider a company’s country as that in which it is domiciled 
(which is likely to be where the headquarters is also). 
• Country of primary listing: While companies can generally list in other 
countries, there tends to be a home bias in their choice of exchange.  
Figure 4 presents the AI use intensity metric by both of these measures of country 
(the company’s domicile and primary exchange listing). Again, the shaded area 
shows the range across all classifications, with the largest countries/exchanges 
singled out. 
German-domiciled companies and the Xetra-listed (Frankfurt stock exchange) 
group record above-average AI-use scores in most quarters since 2022.  
Spain’s AI-use score has risen noticeably over recent quarters and now exceeds 
the aggregate average, reversing its earlier below-average position.  
11 November 2026
Focus Europe

### Page 7

Deutsche Bank AG Page 7

AI-use scores rise across country groups, with indications of faster growth from 
2025. The shared direction suggests a broad European listed-company trend, 
even though differences in score levels and timing persist between countries.

Figure 4: AI use intensity (0-10) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

As a geographical cross-check, our index matches the broad trends of the 
Eurostat ICT annual survey, which places Germany as a clear frontrunner among 
its large peers. But some differences do exist. For instance, Eurostat shows the 
Netherlands with a very high AI adoption. As Figure 5 demonstrates, there is a 
positive correlation between the AI use score metric derived here, and that from 
Eurostat, even though the Eurostat data measures if firms are adopting AI, and 
our new use measure also incorporates the concept of how intensively firms are 
using AI.

11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

Deutsche Bank AG Page 8

Figure 5: Relationship between Eurostat usage and Weighted share of STOXX 
600 mentioning AI usage

Source: Deutsche Bank Research, Eurostat. Notes: each dot represents a country-year pair of AI usage>0

AI productivity impact: Reported benefits are rising, but 
transformational gains remain uncommon 
Higher intensity of use does not necessarily mean firms are benefiting more from 
AI. But our AI productivity impact metric does show a similar pattern to our AI 
usage metric, although it tends to be slightly lower, indicating that companies 
may not yet be reaping the full rewards for their AI investment. 
Financials stand out more clearly on reported productivity benefits than on AI-use 
intensity, while German companies again record relatively strong scores, but only 
in more recent history.

Figure 6: AI's impact on productivity (0-10) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

AI productivity impact across European industries 
Reported AI-productivity benefits have become more prominent across 
industries. Figure 6 shows scores rising gradually during 2022–25, followed by 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 9

Deutsche Bank AG Page 9

indications of a faster increase from late 2025. The profile resembles the AI-use 
trend in Figure 4, suggesting that the stronger adoption narrative is accompanied 
by more discussion of operational gains. 
The aggregate trajectories of the AI use and AI productivity impact scores are 
similar, but sector rankings differ. Technology’s early relative strength in AI use is 
not matched by its productivity score, while financials report above-average 
productivity benefits despite more modest use scores. 
AI productivity impact across countries 
Figure 7 plots the self-report impact of AI on company productivity by country. 
Between 2022-23, German companies recorded average productivity impact, 
even though their AI use score was relatively high. However more recently, their 
AI impact score has persistently been above average. The UK grouping shows 
persistent early outperformance in AI impact during 2022–23, followed by 
convergence towards the aggregate average.

Figure 7: AI's impact on productivity (0-10) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

Figure 8 shows that there is a strong positive correlation between the AI use score 
and the AI productivity score across the STOXX 600 (r-squared = 0.83). The 
relationship between the scores is approximately linear, with a slope of around

0.66: a one-point higher use score is associated with roughly a 0.66-point higher 
productivity score for each country-year.

Source: Deutsche Bank Research

AI labour force impact: On net, AI may be benefiting 
employment, for now… 
Across industries and countries, the recorded score for AI’s impact on the labour 
force remains relatively small. On average, the reported impact on companies’ 
employment has been fairly neutral, with a marginal negative bias over the last 
year or so.

Figure 9: AI's impact on the labour force (-5 to +5) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter. Negative (positive) scores are associated with job losses (gains) 
associated with a company's AI use.

11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 11

Deutsche Bank AG Page 11

AI labour force impact across European industries 
Despite rising productivity, AI has not triggered the negative employment shock 
feared by the public. Aggregate labour impact scores across nearly all sectors 
hover near zero, with a slightly positive balance over history, and a slightly 
negative balance more recently in 2025-26 (Figure 9). But overall the magnitude 
of AI’s impact on labour remains low. For the STOXX 600 aggregate average, the 
score’s maximum deviation from 0 (no impact) is 0.43 on our 0-5 scale. 
AI labour force impact across countries 
Workforce-impact scores are also small across the main country groupings, with 
most countries slowing a slight negative tilt in recent years (Figure 10).

Figure 10: AI impact on the labour force (-5 to +5) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter. Negative (positive) scores are associated with job losses (gains) 
associated with a company's AI use.

The lack of significant impact so far from AI on companies’ labour forces so far is 
consistent with results from our previous work looking at the other side of the 
labour market. Data from our dbDataInsights household survey showed that 
workers across Europe reported a fairly low level of fear about losing their job to 
AI over the next year. The analysis in that report found results that were more 
consistent with workers adapting to the new AI world by upskilling and 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 12

Deutsche Bank AG Page 12

demanding higher wages. Those results would not be consistent with a world in 
which AI was actively replacing labour. 
These findings of a low impact on labour are highly consistent with recent 
academic research (e.g., Yotzov et al., 2026), which found that nearly half of 
surveyed UK and German firms reported absolutely no impact on headcount from 
AI over the past three years, 
Conclusion 
The newly developed framework provides a recurring way to monitor company 
disclosures about AI’s impact on productivity and labour. The central finding of 
this report is a broadening AI transition rather than clear evidence of an 
economy-wide productivity breakthrough. The new metrics indicate that AI use 
remains limited but is rising across the European countries and industries 
represented in the sample. Companies report low but growing productivity gains 
and little aggregate workforce impact so far. This pattern is consistent with a 
technology J-curve, with firms still integrating the new technology, rather than 
proof that this trajectory will unfold. Limited reported workforce effects today do 
not rule out subsequent layoffs or wider organisational restructuring. 
Quarterly updates to this framework will track Europe’s AI transition and provide 
timely signals of whether companies are reporting more substantial productivity 
and employment effects. 
Beyond AI, the framework could be adapted to extract signals about employment 
decisions, investment decisions and the general economic outlook from earnings 
calls. These potential extensions could form the basis of alternative nowcasting 
models for important macroeconomic data; they are applications that will be 
explored in future work.

11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

etc have been redacted.

## Document 466: “坏事全部撞在一起_，美股却逆势反弹：华尔街上演“无票可卖”式逼空！

Source PDF: all report pdf/“坏事全部撞在一起_，美股却逆势反弹：华尔街上演“无票可卖”式逼空！.pdf

### Page 1

英伟达创始⼈⻩仁勋黯然离职、 CPI 与 PPI 连续超预期、美联储放⻛ 12 ⽉加息 50 个基点 ——
利空⻬爆，美股却逆势收涨，标普 500 周涨 2% 。⾼盛数据显⽰对冲基⾦净杠杆降⾄五年最
低，空头基本出清，坏消息砸下却 “ ⽆票可卖 ” ，轧空⾏情推升股市；债市独⾃承压， 2 年期
美债收益率单周飙升 35 个基点。
摘要：
⼀周之内，英伟达创始⼈丑闻缠⾝后黯然离任，美国 CPI 连续第三个⽉超预期， PPI 攀升⾄四年
新⾼，美联储官员放⻛ 12 ⽉加息 50 个基点 —— 所有利空同时引爆，美股却逆势收涨，标普 500 周
涨 2% ，费城半导体指数创 4 ⽉以来最佳单周表现。
答案只有⼀个：空头已⽆牌可打。
这是⼀场典型的 " ⽆票可卖 " 式逼空。⾼盛 Prime 数据显⽰，对冲基⾦在中期选举当周⼤幅削减杠
杆，净多头仓位降⾄五年最低。进⼊本周，空头已基本出清，坏消息砸下来，市场找不到⾜够
的卖盘承接压⼒，股市⾮但没有崩塌，反⽽借助轧空⼀路拉升。与此同时，债市独⾃扛下了所

有的宏观冲击 ——2 年期美债收益率单周飙升 35 个基点，创 2024 年 10 ⽉以来最⼤周度涨幅， 10
年期收益率突破 5.15% 。
然⽽，本周最后两个交易⽇已出现疲态，标普 500 在 100 ⽇均线处遭遇阻⼒，周五股市开始 " 读懂
债市的来信 " 。⾼盛⾼级交易员 Rich Privorotsky 警告：
英伟达 CEO 离任， AI 交易换仓不换⽅向
英伟达 33 年来的灵魂⼈物⻩仁勋的离开，是本周最⼤的⿊天鹅。上周末，⻩仁勋承认婚内出
轨，公司随即启动独⽴调查。周⼆，众议院共和党⼈考虑发出传票，据报道国务卿 Rubio 已就此
警告特朗普，英伟达股价较历史⾼点市值蒸发近 2 万亿美元。
" 市场需要油价企稳、利率回落，⽽这两件事本周⼀件都没发⽣。 "

⽇报》称董事会正准备罢免他。周四上午 8 时 48 分，⻩仁勋正式宣布辞职。 CFO Colette Kress
出任临时 CEO ，前 Synopsys CEO Harvey Jones 出任英伟达史上⾸位独⽴董事⻓。

估值倍数从 30 倍下调⾄ 22 倍，⽬标价降⾄ 220 美元，并援引苹果失去乔布斯后的去估值路径作为
参照。 Bernstein 分析师 Stacy Rasgon 下调评级，在标题为 " 他哪来的时间？ " 的报告中警告，后
续仍会有更多丑闻曝出。
然⽽，英伟达股价当⽇低开约 12% 后收跌仅 4% ⾄ 5% 。据 Vanda Research ，当天英伟达零售买
盘创历史最⾼，约为正常⽔平的 41 倍，散⼾⽤ 30 分钟便完成了对 " 没有⻩仁勋的英伟达 " 的重新定

价。全周英伟达下跌约 11% ，但资⾦并未撤离 AI 赛道，只是换了⽅向： AMD 单周涨约 30% ，博
通涨约 24% ，费城半导体指数录得 4 ⽉以来最佳单周表现。
摩根⼤通分析师 Harlan Sur 将英伟达⽬标价下调⾄ 215 美元；摩根⼤通信⽤团队维持减持评级，
警告最⼤⻛险来⾃英伟达作为供应商、投资⽅或担保⽅参与的 GPU 融资协议。

### Page 3

" ⽆票可卖 " ：仓位出清制造逼空⾏情
本周股市反弹的核⼼逻辑，不在基本⾯，⽽在仓位。⾼盛 Prime 数据显⽰，美国多空对冲基⾦在
中 期 选 举 当 周 将 总 杠 杆 削 减 8.7 个 百 分 点 ， 为 2020 年 3 ⽉ 以 来 最 ⼤ 单 周 降 幅 ； 净 杠 杆 降 ⾄
43.3% ，五年最低。⾃去杠杆开始以来，机构客⼾净卖出科技股超过 300 亿美元，半导体仓位 " ⼏
乎完全回吐了 2026 年的涨幅 " 。
周⼀由此演变为 " 全⾯反弹 " ：标普 500 单⽇涨 2.35% ，为伊朗停⽕以来最⼤单⽇涨幅，纳斯达克
100 涨 3.4% ，费城半导体指数涨 6.8% 。⾼盛场内交易台观察到 " 轻量级的反弹成交 " ，并判断 " 今
天的上涨反映的是供给缺失，⽽⾮买⽅追涨 " 。野村策略师 Charlie McElligott 将其称为 " 史诗级的
波动率压缩 " ，做市商被迫在上涨过程中买⼊。 VIX 全周下跌 27% ⾄ 18.7 ，创⼀年最⼤单周跌
幅。

⾼盛波动率交易台指出，上周四美国市场共成交 5580 万份看跌期权，创历史纪录，随后保护性
头⼨被以史上最快速度之⼀抛售。⾼盛 Lee Coppersmith 指出， VVIX （波动率的波动率）的已
实现波动率在 5 ⽇和 10 ⽇窗⼝均创历史新⾼， " 这是⻛险偏好仍极为脆弱的⼜⼀信号 " 。
系统性多空基⾦录得近三年来最佳两⽇表现，回购项⽬以 1.6 倍于去年的速度运⾏，年初⾄今授
权回购⾦额达 1.5 万亿美元创纪录新⾼。唯⼀的系统性卖⽅是 CTA 策略，⾼盛估计⽆论⾏情⽅
向， CTA 将卖出约 310 亿美元标普期货。

### Page 4

通胀三连热，美联储放⻛加速紧缩
宏观冲击发⽣在周⼆。在 CPI 数据公布前，美联储理事 Chris Waller 在伦敦表⽰，若核⼼ CPI 环⽐
达到 "0.3% 甚⾄更⾼ " ， "12 ⽉会议应当考虑加息 50 个基点 " 。随后数据出炉，核⼼ CPI 约为⾼盛预
估值 0.16% 的两倍， 70 位预测者中仅⼀⼈预判更⾼。
Privorotsky 评价称， " 这份通胀报告毫⽆帮助， CPI 全⾯偏热 …… 强势范围已远超能源，蔓延⾄
医疗服务、保险等领域 …… 很难将其简单归结为能源传导效应，底层通胀被证明具有粘性。 " ⾼
盛经济学家 Mike Cahill 指出，过去四个⽉ CPI 累计超出预测约 32 个基点，且没有任何单⼀分项贡
献超过 4 个基点， " 正是这⼀点在 2021 ⾄ 2022 年让所有⼈措⼿不及 " 。
美联储官员集体加码。达拉斯联储主席 Lorie Logan 表⽰ FOMC" 需要⾄少再加息两次 25 个基
点 " ；理事 Bowman 表⽰⽀持 12 ⽉加息；旧⾦⼭联储主席 Daly 称 " 倾向于进⼀步收紧 " ；克利夫兰

联储主席 Hammack 称 " 需要将政策调整⾄限制性⽔平 " ；芝加哥联储主席 Goolsbee 警告 " 我们正在
逼近历史上对任何央⾏⽽⾔都极为棘⼿的区域 " ，并将数据中⼼建设热潮形容为 " 历史规模的财政
刺激 " 。
⾼盛经济学家随即将 2027 年 3 ⽉新增⼀次加息，预测联邦基⾦利率峰值达 4.25% ⾄ 4.50% ，⾸次
降息推迟⾄ 2027 年 12 ⽉。野村做出类似调整，市场⽬前定价 12 ⽉加息 50 个基点的概率约为
10% ，明年 1 ⽉再加 25 个基点的概率约为 70% 。
周五 PPI 进⼀步添压：⽣产者价格同⽐上涨 6.5% ，为 2022 年 11 ⽉以来最⾼；核⼼ PPI 同⽐涨
5.0% 。⽩宫经济顾问委员会主席 Christopher Phelan 试图淡化 50 个基点⼤幅加息的预期，但债
市和股市均未买账。

### Page 5

债市独扛冲击， 30 年期收益率触及新⾼
⽤⼀位债券资深交易员的话说，周⼆是 " 彻底的杀戮 " 。
2 年期美债收益率当⽇跳升 17.5 个基点，为 2024 年 10 ⽉以来最⼤单⽇涨幅。供给压⼒雪上加霜：
财政部在假⽇前集中安排再融资拍卖，三期全部遭遇 " 尾差 " （成交收益率⾼于⼆级市场）。周⼀ 3
年期拍卖尾差为 5 ⽉以来最⼤；周⼆ 10 年期拍卖尾差为 2 ⽉以来最⼤，间接投标⽐例从 80.3% 骤
降⾄ 61.2% ，为 2023 年 10 ⽉以来最低；周四 30 年期拍卖成交收益率 5.441% ，尾差为 6 ⽉以来最
⼤，⼀级⾃营商被迫持有的⽐例亦为 6 ⽉以来最多。
全周⽽⾔， 2 年期收益率累计上升约 35 个基点， 10 年期约 28 个基点， 30 年期约 20 个基点。 10 年
期收益率突破 5.15% ，距多年期⾼点仅约 15 个基点。 5 ⾄ 30 年期收益率曲线熊式平坦化约 13 个基
点。

本轮周期⾼点，三个⽉内⾛阔 270 个基点，⽽ BB 级利差反⽽收窄。周⼆受美债抛售拖累，投资

### Page 8

然⽽随后局势急转直下。霍尔⽊兹海峡⼏乎每天都有油轮触雷，伊斯兰⾰命卫队袭击了阿曼迪
达⻢尔岛上引导船只通过海峡南航道的导航设备，伊朗官员表⽰不会将 " 哪怕⼀个原⼦ " 的浓缩铀
移出国境。周四，就在《华尔街⽇报》报道副总统 Vance 正考虑与伊朗直接会谈后不久，特朗普
关上了外交⼤⻔，明确表⽰ " 我们不需要达成协议 " ，并追加称 " 我们已经拿⾛了他们的钱，数千
亿美元，还有他们的海峡！ " 油价随即⼤幅反弹。
⾼盛 EMEA 团队简短评价： " 感觉去紧张化已经死透了。 " 周五国际能源署表⽰⽯油市场正转向供
应过剩，限制了油价涨幅，布伦特原油收于每桶 96 美元附近， WTI 约 89 美元，周度温和下跌。
资⾦流向：撤出货币基⾦，涌⼊欧洲股市
LSEG Lipper 最新数据显⽰，全球股票基⾦当周净流⼊ 510 亿美元，为九⽉底以来最⼤单周流
⼊，连续第⼋周录得净流⼊。其中欧洲股票基⾦流⼊ 351 亿美元，创 2021 年 6 ⽉以来最⾼，斯托
克 600 指数同期多次创历史新⾼；美国股票基⾦流⼊ 66 亿美元，亚洲基⾦流⼊ 62 亿美元。

美国国内，股票基⾦连续第⼆周录得净流⼊，但 66 亿美元的规模不及前⼀周 150 亿美元的⼀半。
⼤盘股基⾦吸纳 116 亿美元，中⼩盘基⾦合计流出 35 亿美元，⾏业主题基⾦流出 25 亿美元。货币
市场基⾦流出 375 亿美元，回吐此前⼀周创 2020 年 4 ⽉以来最⼤单周流⼊的部分资⾦；债券基⾦
⼩幅流出 5 亿美元，七周连续流⼊态势就此中断。
流向格局清晰：资⾦正在回流股市，但⽬的地是欧洲和⼤盘股，⽽⾮美国市场中⻛险更⾼的部
分。
